COLDWAR_CZE_1970_calculate_budget_streams = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_programmes_weekly_expense = 0 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_projects_weekly_expense = 0 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_bilateral_weekly_expense = 0 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_bilateral_weekly_income = 0 }
	if = { limit = { IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes country_exists = CZE CZE = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_initialized } }
	COLDWAR_CZE_1970_bilateral_calculate_streams = yes
	if = { limit = { tag = CZE } COLDWAR_CZE_1970_calculate_programme_streams = yes COLDWAR_CZE_1970_projects_calculate_streams = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_CZE_1970_init = {
	if = {
	limit = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_is_scenario = yes tag = CZE NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_initialized } }
	set_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_initialized
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_normalisation = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_soviet_presence = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_federal_tension = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_engineering = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_soft_budgets = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_social_compact = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_coal_legacy = 1 }
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_academic_legacy = 1 }
	COLDWAR_CZE_1970_politics_init = yes
	COLDWAR_CZE_1970_projects_init = yes
	COLDWAR_CZE_1970_bilateral_init = yes
	}
	if = { limit = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_is_scenario = yes tag = CZE } COLDWAR_CZE_1970_refresh_spirits = yes }
}

	if = {

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_earned_ksc_husak NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_ksc_husak } } complete_national_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_ksc_husak }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_earned_husak_housing NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_husak_housing } } complete_national_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_husak_housing }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_earned_husak_consumer NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_husak_consumer } } complete_national_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_husak_consumer }

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_earned_husak_invest NOT = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_husak_invest } } complete_national_focus = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_husak_invest }

		add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_programmes_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_policy_quote tooltip = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_weekly_expense_tt }
	}

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { COLDWAR_CZE_1970_slot_ksc_current_programme_1 = 2 } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_husak_settlement }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_schools } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_schools }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_bloc } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_bloc }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_surveillance } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_surveillance }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_nomenklatura } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_nomenklatura }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_settlement } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_bilak_settlement }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_strougal_managers } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_strougal_managers }

	if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_strougal_surplus } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_strougal_surplus }

		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_state_retain add_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_state_purge }

		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_start_2 }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_start_2 add_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_central }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_republics }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_republics add_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_central }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_permanent }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_permanent add_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_central }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_confederation }
		swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_confederation add_idea = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_central }
	}
	else = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_CZE_1970_spirit_fed_central }
}
